<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="a_007"></span>INNOVATIVE EYEWEAR, INC.</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTES TO THE CONDENSED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>March&#160;31, 2026 and 2025 (Unaudited)</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000374" name="us-gaap:NatureOfOperations"><p id="xdx_80F_eus-gaap--NatureOfOperations_zjBhygWtvPf5" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE 1 &#8211; <span id="xdx_825_z0fyQVYii8a2">GENERAL INFORMATION</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Company Description</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Innovative
Eyewear, Inc. (the &#8220;Company,&#8221; &#8220;us,&#8221; &#8220;we,&#8221; or &#8220;our&#8221;), is a corporation organized under
the laws of the State of Florida that develops and sells cutting-edge smart eyewear &#8211; including prescription eyeglasses, ready-to-wear
sunglasses, safety glasses, and sport glasses &#8211; which are designed to allow our customers to remain connected to their digital
lives. We sell smart eyewear under our own Lucyd brand, which includes the Lucyd Lyte<sup>&#174;</sup> and Lucyd Armor product lines,
as well as cobranded smart eyewear under the Nautica<sup>&#174;</sup> <i>Powered by Luycd</i>, Eddie Bauer<sup>&#174;</sup> <i>Powered by
Luycd</i>, and Reebok<sup>&#174;</sup> <i>Powered by Luycd</i> product lines.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The Company was originally founded by Lucyd Ltd., a portfolio company of Tekcapital Plc through Tekcapital Europe, Ltd. (collectively, together with Lucyd Ltd., &#8220;Tekcapital and Affiliates&#8221;), which owned approximately 4% of our issued and outstanding shares of common stock as of March&#160;31, 2026.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000376" name="us-gaap:BasisOfPresentationAndSignificantAccountingPoliciesTextBlock"><p id="xdx_842_eus-gaap--BasisOfPresentationAndSignificantAccountingPoliciesTextBlock_zac1lTu2XA9f" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i><span id="xdx_86E_zTFrOZJa9qR5">Basis of Presentation</span></i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The accompanying condensed balance sheet as of December&#160;31, 2025 (which has been derived from audited financial statements) and the unaudited interim condensed financial statements have been prepared in accordance with accounting principles generally accepted in the United States (&#8220;GAAP&#8221;) for interim financial information and pursuant to the instructions to Form 10-Q and Article 8 of Regulation S-X promulgated by the United States Securities and Exchange Commission (&#8220;SEC&#8221;). Certain information or footnote disclosures normally included in financial statements prepared in accordance with GAAP have been condensed or omitted, pursuant to the rules and regulations of the SEC for interim financial reporting. Accordingly, they do not include all the information and footnotes necessary for a comprehensive presentation of financial position, results of operations, or cash flows. These unaudited condensed financial statements should be read in conjunction with the Company&#8217;s Annual Report on Form 10-K for the year ended December&#160;31, 2025, as filed with the SEC on March&#160;25, 2026.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In the opinion of management, all adjustments considered necessary for the fair presentation of the financial statements for the periods presented have been included. The results of operations for the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026 are not necessarily indicative of the results to be expected for future periods or the full year.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000378" name="us-gaap:PriorPeriodReclassificationAdjustmentDescription"><p id="xdx_849_eus-gaap--PriorPeriodReclassificationAdjustmentDescription_ziZel5MteiXa" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i><span id="xdx_863_zlbVRvme70W2">Reclassifications</span></i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Certain prior period amounts have been reclassified to conform to current period presentation &#8211; specifically, $5,423 of nonemployee stock-based payment expense for the three months ended March&#160;31, 2025 has been reclassified to a separate line on the condensed statement of cash flows, rather than aggregated with stock-based compensation.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000380" name="us-gaap:UseOfEstimates"><p id="xdx_84F_eus-gaap--UseOfEstimates_z6jKlhvzp1w9" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i><span id="xdx_861_zeqw9xxoD68b">Use of Estimates</span></i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The preparation of financial statements in conformity with GAAP requires management to make estimates and assumptions that affect the reported amounts of assets and liabilities and disclosure of contingent assets and liabilities at the date of the financial statements and the reported amounts of revenues and expenses during the reporting period. Actual results could differ from those estimates, particularly given the significant uncertainties associated with the current state of international trade and the overall economic environment.</span></p>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_238_z8UvHP0sHgvj" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p></ix:exclude>

    <div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><p style="margin: 0pt">&#160;</p></div>
    <!-- Field: /Page --></ix:exclude>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000383" name="us-gaap:SegmentReportingDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_843_eus-gaap--SegmentReportingDisclosureTextBlock_z4Cd8MKbDJ33" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i><span id="xdx_866_zBZNIRjQEEgg">Segment Reporting</span></i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The Company has a single reportable segment, which generates revenue from the sales of smartglasses, and related accessories and apps. The Company derives revenue primarily in North America and manages its business activities on a consolidated basis. The accounting policies of our single reportable segment are the same as those for the Company as a whole.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The Company&#8217;s chief operating decision maker, as such term is defined under GAAP, is our Chief Executive Officer. The chief operating decision maker assesses performance for the single reportable segment and decides how to allocate resources based on net income that also is reported on the income statement as consolidated net income. The measure of segment assets is reported on the balance sheet as total consolidated assets. The Company does not have intra-entity sales or transfers.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric></ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000385" name="lucy:LiquidityTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80A_ecustom--LiquidityTextBlock_zjgxDmowohb3" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE 2 &#8211; <span id="xdx_821_zhFxERlyo24h">LIQUIDITY</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The Company&#8217;s business and operations are sensitive to general business and economic conditions in the United States. A host of factors beyond the Company&#8217;s control could cause fluctuations in these conditions. Adverse conditions may include recession, downturn, or otherwise, changes in regulations or restrictions in imports, competition, or changes in consumer taste. These adverse conditions could affect the Company&#8217;s financial condition and the results of its operations.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The Company meets its day-to-day working capital requirements using monies raised through sales of eyewear and issuances of equity. During the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026, the Company raised approximately $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--ProceedsFromIssuanceOrSaleOfEquity_pn3n3_dm_c20260101__20260331_z98zoWXyjiP1" title="Cash proceeds through the issuance of equity"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOrSaleOfEquity" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000387" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="6" unitRef="USD">1.5</ix:nonFraction></span> million of aggregate gross proceeds through at-the-market offerings of common stock (see Note 11 for details).&#160;The Company has also entered into an agreement with a related party, under which the Company may make borrowings of up to $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--ShorttermDebtMaximumAmountOutstandingDuringPeriod_pn3n3_dm_c20260101__20260331_z56PQiSOsrt1" title="Maximum borrowing amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShorttermDebtMaximumAmountOutstandingDuringPeriod" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000389" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="6" unitRef="USD">1.25</ix:nonFraction></span> million (see Note 8 for details); as of March&#160;31, 2026, the Company has not borrowed any amounts under such agreement. The Company&#8217;s forecasts and projections indicate that the Company expects to have sufficient liquidity to fund operations through at least the next 12 months. However, the Company may raise additional funds if management believes it would be beneficial to do so.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000391" name="us-gaap:AccountsAndNontradeReceivableTextBlock"><p id="xdx_804_eus-gaap--AccountsAndNontradeReceivableTextBlock_zaagGOf0oU91" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE 3 &#8211; <span id="xdx_821_zkDxH2iTk35a">REVENUE AND ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Revenue</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Our revenue is primarily generated from the sales of prescription and non-prescription optical glasses and sunglasses, and shipping charges which are charged to the customer associated with these purchases. We sell products through our retail store resellers, distributors, on our own website Lucyd.co, and on Amazon.com. We have also recently started to generate revenue from the sale of subscriptions to the &#8220;Pro&#8221; version of our Lucyd app, which provides unlimited ChatGPT interactions and priority tech support for a monthly or annual fee.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><p id="xdx_8A1_z6Kx9gqKjaNb" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In instances where the collectability of contractual consideration is not probable at the time of sale, the revenue is deferred on our balance sheet as a contract liability, and the associated cost of goods sold is deferred on our balance sheet as a contract asset; subsequently, we recognize such revenue and cost of goods sold as payments are received. With respect to such instances, during the three months ended March&#160;31, 2025, we recognized $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--DeferredRevenueRevenueRecognized1_c20250101__20250331_pp0p" title="Revenue recognized"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DeferredRevenueRevenueRecognized1" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000411" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">7,500</ix:nonFraction></span> of revenue that was included in the contract liability balance of $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--ContractWithCustomerLiability_c20250102_pp0p" title="Contract liability"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ContractWithCustomerLiability" contextRef="AsOf2025-01-02" id="Fact000413" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">47,950</ix:nonFraction></span> as of January&#160;1, 2025. There was no comparable balance of such deferred revenue as of December&#160;31, 2025 or March&#160;31, 2026, and no such revenue recognized or deferred during the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The balance of revenue for orders received but not yet fulfilled that has been deferred on our balance sheet as a contract liability was $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--ContractWithCustomerLiability_c20260331_pp0p" title="Contract liability"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ContractWithCustomerLiability" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000416" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">9,336</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--ContractWithCustomerLiability_c20251231_pp0p" title="Contract liability"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ContractWithCustomerLiability" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000418" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">55,383</ix:nonFraction></span> as of March&#160;31, 2026 and December&#160;31, 2025, respectively. During the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026, we recognized $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--DeferredRevenueRevenueRecognized1_c20260101__20260331_pp0p" title="Revenue recognized"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DeferredRevenueRevenueRecognized1" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000420" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">55,383</ix:nonFraction></span> of revenue related to these instances, all of which was included in the contract liability balance of $<span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--ContractWithCustomerLiability_c20260102_pp0p" title="Contract liability"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ContractWithCustomerLiability" contextRef="AsOf2026-01-02" id="Fact000422" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">55,383</ix:nonFraction></span> as of January&#160;1, 2026.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The balance of unearned revenue related to app subscriptions that has been deferred on our balance sheet as a contract liability was $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--ContractWithCustomerLiability_c20260331__srt--ProductOrServiceAxis__custom--SubscriptionsMember_pp0p" title="Contract liability"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ContractWithCustomerLiability" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_SubscriptionsMember" id="Fact000424" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">5,633</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--ContractWithCustomerLiability_c20251231__srt--ProductOrServiceAxis__custom--SubscriptionsMember_pp0p" title="Contract liability"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ContractWithCustomerLiability" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_SubscriptionsMember" id="Fact000426" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">4,506</ix:nonFraction></span> as of March&#160;31, 2026 and December&#160;31, 2025, respectively. During the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026 and 2025, we recognized $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--DeferredRevenueRevenueRecognized1_c20260101__20260331__srt--ProductOrServiceAxis__custom--SubscriptionsMember_pp0p" title="Revenue recognized"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DeferredRevenueRevenueRecognized1" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_SubscriptionsMember" id="Fact000428" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,802</ix:nonFraction></span>&#160;and $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--DeferredRevenueRevenueRecognized1_c20250101__20250331__srt--ProductOrServiceAxis__custom--SubscriptionsMember_pp0p" title="Revenue recognized"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DeferredRevenueRevenueRecognized1" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31_custom_SubscriptionsMember" id="Fact000430" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,030</ix:nonFraction></span> of revenue, respectively, that was included in the contract liability balance of $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--ContractWithCustomerLiability_iI_pp0d_c20260102__srt--ProductOrServiceAxis__custom--SubscriptionsMember_zv16NNRSxynb" title="Contract liability"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ContractWithCustomerLiability" contextRef="AsOf2026-01-02_custom_SubscriptionsMember" id="Fact000432" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">4,506</ix:nonFraction></span> as of January&#160;1, 2026 and $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--ContractWithCustomerLiability_iI_pp0d_c20250102__srt--ProductOrServiceAxis__custom--SubscriptionsMember_zIeFqEC9Ilzb" title="Contract liability"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ContractWithCustomerLiability" contextRef="AsOf2025-01-02_custom_SubscriptionsMember" id="Fact000434" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">2,401</ix:nonFraction></span> as of January&#160;1, 2025, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">For all of our product sales, at the time of sale, we establish a reserve for returns, based on historical experience and expected future returns as well as review of individual returns received in the month following the balance sheet date; such reserve is recorded as a reduction of sales. The Company recorded an allowance for sales returns of $<span id="xdx_90D_ecustom--AllowanceForSalesReturns_c20260331_pp0p" title="Allowance for sales returns"><ix:nonFraction name="lucy:AllowanceForSalesReturns" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000436" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">12,400</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_90C_ecustom--AllowanceForSalesReturns_c20251231_pp0p" title="Allowance for sales returns"><ix:nonFraction name="lucy:AllowanceForSalesReturns" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000438" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">14,669</ix:nonFraction></span> as March&#160;31, 2026 and December&#160;31, 2025, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Accounts Receivable</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Accounts receivable are uncollateralized obligations due from customers under normal trade terms. For direct-to-consumer sales, payment is required before product is shipped. For wholesale orders, we offer &#8220;net 30&#8221; payment terms on wholesale orders of $1,500 or more in accordance with industry standards. The Company, by policy, routinely assesses the financial strength of its customers.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Accounts receivable are reported at the amount billed to the customer, net of an allowance for credit losses. The allowance for credit losses is determined based upon a variety of judgments and factors. Factors considered in determining the allowance include historical collection, write-off experience, and management&#8217;s assessment of collectibility from customers, including current conditions, reasonable forecasts, and expectations of future collectability and collection efforts. Management continuously assesses the collectability of receivables and adjusts estimates based on actual experience and future expectations. Receivable balances are written-off against the allowance when such balances are deemed to be uncollectable.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">A roll forward of the allowance for credit losses for the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026 and 2025 is as follows:</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000462" name="us-gaap:InventoryDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_803_eus-gaap--InventoryDisclosureTextBlock_zSd20b4rR7jj" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE 4 &#8211; <span id="xdx_828_z5gjQZRsdGSc">INVENTORY</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Our inventory predominantly consists of purchased eyewear and related accessories, and is stated at the lower of cost or net realizable value, with cost determined on a specific identification method of inventory costing which attaches the actual cost to an identifiable unit of product. Also included within inventory as of both at March&#160;31, 2026 and December&#160;31, 2025 was $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--InventoryNet_iI_pp0d_c20260331__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__custom--ThirdPartySupplierMember_z9Bnel5IBPP7" title="Inventory"><span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--InventoryNet_iI_pp0d_c20251231__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__custom--ThirdPartySupplierMember_z7uDlQO12bfi" title="Inventory"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InventoryNet" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_ThirdPartySupplierMember" id="Fact000464" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InventoryNet" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_ThirdPartySupplierMember" id="Fact000466" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">72,864</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> of electronic components purchased from a third-party supplier for use by our manufacturer in their future production of our eyewear.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Provisions for excess, obsolete, or slow-moving inventory are recorded after periodic evaluation of historical sales, current economic trends, forecasted sales, estimated product life cycles, and estimated inventory levels. Such provisions were $<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--InventoryWriteDown_pp0d_c20260101__20260331_z1C9fUK7Egsj" title="Provisions for excess, obsolete, or slow-moving inventory"><span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--InventoryWriteDown_pp0d_c20250101__20251231_zABXWMepvgej" title="Provisions for excess, obsolete, or slow-moving inventory"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InventoryWriteDown" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000468" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InventoryWriteDown" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-12-31" id="Fact000470" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">59,000</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> as of both March&#160;31, 2026 and December&#160;31, 2025.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Our non-product inventory consists of virtual try-on kiosks, modular display systems, and interactive LCD fixtures that have not yet been deployed at retailers, as well as certain accessories that are intended to be used as promotional items, rather than sold in the normal course of business. These items are presented in the condensed balance sheet within Other current assets as of March&#160;31, 2026. The balance of such non-product inventory was $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--OtherInventory_c20260331_pp0p" title="Non-product inventory"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherInventory" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000472" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">72,575</ix:nonFraction></span> as of March&#160;31, 2026.</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000475" name="us-gaap:InvestmentTextBlock"><p id="xdx_806_eus-gaap--InvestmentTextBlock_z58dUYi6wwk" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE 5 &#8211; <span id="xdx_82E_zeAQ5AOWfOvf">INVESTMENTS</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">All highly liquid investments with original maturities of three months or less, including money market funds, certificates of deposit, and U.S. Treasury bills purchased three months or less from maturity, are considered cash equivalents.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">As of January&#160;1, 2025, the Company held investments in U.S. Treasury bills, which were purchased in September&#160;2024 and matured in March&#160;2025. These investments were classified as &#8220;held-to-maturity&#8221; and were recorded at amortized cost. Upon maturity of these investments, the Company recognized a realized gain $<span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--UnrealizedGainLossOnInvestments_pp0d_c20250101__20250331_zqigE6yUKRok" title="Unrealized gain"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:UnrealizedGainLossOnInvestments" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000477" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">104,816</ix:nonFraction></span> for the three months ended March&#160;31, 2025.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">There were no amounts classified as &#8220;held-to-maturity&#8221; investments as of December&#160;31, 2025.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">As
of March&#160;31, 2026, the Company held investments in U.S. Treasury bills, which were purchased in January&#160;2026 and matured
in April&#160;2026. During the three months ended March 31, 2026, the Company recognized $<span id="xdx_90A_ecustom--InterestIncomeRelatedToInvestments_c20260101__20260331_zXnibyhz2pd5" title="Interest income related to investments"><ix:nonFraction name="lucy:InterestIncomeRelatedToInvestments" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000479" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">10,393</ix:nonFraction></span> of interest income related to these
investments. These investments were classified as &#8220;held-to-maturity&#8221; and were recorded at amortized cost of $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--AmortizationOfAcquisitionCosts_pp0d_c20260101__20260331_zNDWYmHPqI56" title="Amortized cost"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AmortizationOfAcquisitionCosts" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000481" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,298,859</ix:nonFraction></span>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000548" name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80C_eus-gaap--IncomeTaxDisclosureTextBlock_zx0gLDibwjKd" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE 7 &#8211; <span id="xdx_82C_zPw74ErlUPj3">INCOME TAXES</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">At the end of each interim reporting period, the Company estimates its effective tax rate expected to be applied for the full year. This estimate is used to determine the income tax provision or benefit on a year-to-date basis and may change in subsequent interim periods. The Company has <span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--IncomeTaxExpenseBenefit_pp0d_do_c20260101__20260331_zc5KZq3MW1uh" title="Income tax provision"><span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--IncomeTaxExpenseBenefit_pp0d_do_c20250101__20250331_zW1M68mh3vvd" title="Income tax provision"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxExpenseBenefit" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000550" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxExpenseBenefit" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000552" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">no</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span>t recorded an income tax provision or benefit for the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026 and 2025 as it maintains a full valuation allowance against its net deferred tax assets.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000554" name="us-gaap:RelatedPartyTransactionsDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_801_eus-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsDisclosureTextBlock_zjphuaWHIDP2" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE 8 &#8211; <span id="xdx_828_z6Hra6CKHLrb">RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS AND AGREEMENTS</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Management Service Agreement</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The Company is party to a management services agreement with Tekcapital Europe, Ltd. (an affiliate of Lucyd Ltd., whose Chief Executive Officer is the father of our Chief Executive Officer), which was originally entered into in 2020 and subsequently amended in 2022. While the agreement does not stipulate a specific maturity date, it can be terminated with 30 calendar days written notice by any party.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Under this agreement, the related party provides the following services to us, for which we are billed $35,000 per quarter:</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 0.25in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 0.25in; text-align: left"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#9679;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Research, technical and legal review, recruitment, software development, marketing, public relations, and advertisement; and</span></td> </tr>
  </table>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%">
  <tr style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 0.25in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 0.25in; text-align: left"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#9679;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Advice, assistance, and consultation services to support the Company or in relation to any other related matter.</span></td> </tr>
  </table>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">During the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026 and 2025, the Company incurred $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--ManagementFeeExpense_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--TekcapitalEuropeLtdMember_pp0p" title="Management fee"><span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--ManagementFeeExpense_c20250101__20250331__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--TekcapitalEuropeLtdMember_pp0p" title="Management fee"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ManagementFeeExpense" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_TekcapitalEuropeLtdMember" id="Fact000556" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ManagementFeeExpense" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31_custom_TekcapitalEuropeLtdMember" id="Fact000558" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">35,000</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> in each respective period under the management services agreement.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Rent of Office Space</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Since 2022, under an agreement between the Company and Tekcapital Europe, Ltd, Tekcapital bills the Company for an allocation of rent paid by Tekcapital on the Company&#8217;s behalf. The underlying lease between Tekcapital and its landlord has an end date of January&#160;31, 2027. The Company recognized $<span id="xdx_901_ecustom--RentExpenses_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--TekcapitalEuropeLtdMember_pp0p" title="Rent expenses"><ix:nonFraction name="lucy:RentExpenses" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_TekcapitalEuropeLtdMember" id="Fact000560" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">25,601</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_907_ecustom--RentExpenses_c20250101__20250331__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--TekcapitalEuropeLtdMember_pp0p" title="Rent expenses"><ix:nonFraction name="lucy:RentExpenses" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31_custom_TekcapitalEuropeLtdMember" id="Fact000562" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">39,653</ix:nonFraction></span> of expense related to this month-to-month arrangement for the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026 and 2025, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Loan to Tekcapital Europe, Ltd.</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On December&#160;19, 2025, the Company entered into an intercompany loan agreement (as lender) with Tekcapital Europe, Ltd. (as borrower) and Tekcapital Plc, the parent of Tekcapital Europe, Ltd. Pursuant to this agreement, the Company agreed to make a loan facility available to Tekcapital Europe, Ltd. for up to a maximum of $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--LoansPayable_c20251219__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--TekcapitalEuropeLtd.Member_pp0p" title="Intercompany loan amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LoansPayable" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-19_custom_TekcapitalEuropeLtd.Member" id="Fact000564" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">300,000</ix:nonFraction></span>. Tekcapital Europe, Ltd. was able to receive advances under this facility upon request through January&#160;19, 2026; any amounts advanced to Tekcapital Europe, Ltd. bore simple interest at a rate of&#160;<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentInterestRateDuringPeriod_c20251201__20251219__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--TekcapitalEuropeLtd.Member_pd" title="Interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentInterestRateDuringPeriod" contextRef="From2025-12-012025-12-19_custom_TekcapitalEuropeLtd.Member" id="Fact000566" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Ratio">12</ix:nonFraction>%</span>&#160;per annum, and were required to be repaid on or before&#160;March&#160;19, 2026.&#160;Tekcapital Plc executed the agreement as guarantor for Tekcapital Europe, Ltd. on the full amount of the loan. On December&#160;23, 2025, Tekcapital Europe, Ltd. borrowed $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--LoansPayable_c20251223__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--TekcapitalEuropeLtd.Member_pp0p" title="Intercompany loan amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LoansPayable" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-23_custom_TekcapitalEuropeLtd.Member" id="Fact000568" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">300,000</ix:nonFraction></span>&#160;under this agreement; the $300,000 outstanding principal balance of this loan, plus accrued interest receivable of $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--InterestReceivableCurrent_c20251223__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--TekcapitalEuropeLtd.Member_pp0p" title="Accrued interest receivable"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InterestReceivableCurrent" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-23_custom_TekcapitalEuropeLtd.Member" id="Fact000570" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">789</ix:nonFraction></span>, was included within Due from Tekcapital and Affiliates in the accompanying condensed balance sheet as of December&#160;31, 2025.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On February&#160;24, 2026, Tekcapital Europe, Ltd. repaid in full all of the outstanding balance of the loan that the Company had made to Tekcapital on December&#160;19, 2025. The total amount paid to the Company was $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--RepaymentsOfDebt_c20260201__20260224__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--TekcapitalEuropeLtdMember_pp0p" title="Repayment of debt"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:RepaymentsOfDebt" contextRef="From2026-02-012026-02-24_custom_TekcapitalEuropeLtdMember" id="Fact000572" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">306,115</ix:nonFraction></span>, of which $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentRepaidPrincipal_c20260201__20260224__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--TekcapitalEuropeLtdMember_pp0p" title="Repayment of principal"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentRepaidPrincipal" contextRef="From2026-02-012026-02-24_custom_TekcapitalEuropeLtdMember" id="Fact000574" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">300,000</ix:nonFraction></span> represented the principal amount and $<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentPeriodicPaymentInterest_c20260201__20260224__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--TekcapitalEuropeLtdMember_pp0p" title="Repayment of accrued interest"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentPeriodicPaymentInterest" contextRef="From2026-02-012026-02-24_custom_TekcapitalEuropeLtdMember" id="Fact000576" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">6,115</ix:nonFraction></span> represented accrued interest. As of March&#160;31, 2026, no amounts remained outstanding or payable to us under this agreement.</span></p>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_23C_z0bDUIu1g0O1" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p></ix:exclude>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Lucyd Ltd. Financing Agreement</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On March&#160;1, 2024, the Company entered into an agreement with Lucyd Ltd. pursuant to which the Company could receive up to $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--LoansPayable_c20240301__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--LucydLtdMember_pp0p" title="Intercompany loan amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LoansPayable" contextRef="AsOf2024-03-01_custom_LucydLtdMember" id="Fact000579" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,250,000</ix:nonFraction></span> either (a) in services provided by Lucyd Ltd. to the Company or (b) in cash upon request of funds by the Company. Once funds or services are received by the Company, it will issue a convertible note to Lucyd Ltd. that will bear interest at <span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentInterestRateDuringPeriod_c20240228__20240301__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--LucydLtdMember_pd" title="Interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentInterestRateDuringPeriod" contextRef="From2024-02-282024-03-01_custom_LucydLtdMember" id="Fact000581" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Ratio">10</ix:nonFraction>%</span> per annum and include the option to convert the note into shares of the Company&#8217;s common stock upon certain defined events. Upon issuance, the convertible note would have had a maturity date of September&#160;1, 2025 (subsequently amended, as described below), at which time all outstanding principal and accrued interest, if any, would be payable in full in cash or in the Company&#8217;s common stock. The Company would be able to prepay the convertible notes at any time with the written consent of Lucyd Ltd.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On March&#160;1, 2025, the Company and Lucyd Ltd. entered into an amendment of this agreement, such that upon issuance, the convertible note would have a maturity date of September&#160;1, 2026. There were no other changes to the terms and provisions of the agreement.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On March&#160;11, 2026, the Company and Lucyd Ltd. entered into a further amendment of this agreement, such that upon issuance, the convertible note will have a maturity date of September&#160;1, 2027. There were no other changes to the terms and provisions of the agreement.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The Company has not borrowed any amounts under this agreement with Lucyd Ltd.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>License Agreements</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In 2022 and 2023, we entered into various multi-year license agreements which grant us the right to sell certain branded smart eyewear, including the Nautica, Eddie Bauer, and Reebok brands worldwide. These agreements require us to pay royalties based on a percentage of net retail and wholesale sales during the period of the license, and also require guaranteed minimum royalty payments. The agreements have base terms of 10 years but are cancellable at the option of the Company during the fifth year (i.e., 2027 and 2028).</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The aggregate future minimum payments due under these license agreements are as follows:</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The Company recognized $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--OtherCostAndExpenseOperating_c20260101__20260331_pp0p" title="Expenses related to license agreements"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherCostAndExpenseOperating" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000601" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">208,500</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--OtherCostAndExpenseOperating_c20250101__20250331_pp0p" title="Expenses related to license agreements"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherCostAndExpenseOperating" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000603" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">133,336</ix:nonFraction></span> of expense related to all license agreements for the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026 and 2025, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Long-Term Payment Plan for Information Technology System and Services</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The Company has entered into a long-term payment plan agreement with Oracle for the payment of costs related to the implementation of the Company&#8217;s new ERP system (which went live in April&#160;2025) and related cloud services. Under this agreement, the Company is obligated to make payments of $4,035 per month through July&#160;2027. As of March&#160;31, 2026, the Company&#8217;s remaining obligation under this arrangement was $<span id="xdx_90C_ecustom--Obligations_c20260331_pp0p" title="Obligations"><ix:nonFraction name="lucy:Obligations" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000605" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">68,902</ix:nonFraction></span>, of which $<span id="xdx_90F_ecustom--Obligations_iI_pp0d_c20260331__custom--BalanceSheetsLocationAxis__custom--AccountsPayableAndAccruedExpensesMember_zL38V5LzWvl9" title="Obligations"><ix:nonFraction name="lucy:Obligations" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_AccountsPayableAndAccruedExpensesMember" id="Fact000607" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">48,424</ix:nonFraction></span> is included within Accounts payable and accrued expenses in the accompanying condensed balance sheet, and $<span id="xdx_90A_ecustom--Obligations_iI_pp0d_c20260331__custom--BalanceSheetsLocationAxis__custom--NoncurrentLiabilitiesMember_zqdevEqNznz2" title="Obligations"><ix:nonFraction name="lucy:Obligations" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_NoncurrentLiabilitiesMember" id="Fact000609" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">20,478</ix:nonFraction></span> is reflected within Non-Current Liabilities in the accompanying condensed balance sheet.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Leases</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Our executive offices are located at 11900 Biscayne Blvd., Suite 630 Miami, Florida 33181. Our executive offices are provided to us by a related party (see Note 8). We consider our current office space adequate for our current operations.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Other Commitments</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">See related party management services agreement discussed in Note 8.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>Legal Matters</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">We are not currently the subject of any material pending legal proceedings; however, we may from time to time become a party to various legal proceedings arising in the ordinary course of business.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>International Trade and Tariffs</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Beginning in April&#160;2025, the U.S. government announced new or increased tariffs on goods imported from various countries to the U.S., and countries subject to such tariffs have imposed or may in the future impose retaliatory tariffs and other trade measures. These developments negatively impacted our results of operations during the year ended December&#160;31, 2025. Management has taken various actions to mitigate the negative impacts of tariffs on our financial results; these actions have been largely successful thus far. However, the current international geopolitical climate related to tariffs is fluid and continues to evolve, and we cannot provide any assurance that actions that have been taken by management or that may be taken by management in the future will be successful in future periods. As such, we cannot predict with certainty the ultimate impacts that international trade and tariff developments may have on our business and financial results in the future, but those impacts could be material.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In February&#160;2026, the U.S. Supreme Court issued a ruling invalidating some of the tariffs imposed by the U.S. government; however, the ultimate availability, timing, and amount of any potential refunds of such tariffs remain highly uncertain and are subject to further legal, regulatory, and administrative developments. As such, we cannot reasonably estimate the impact, if any, on our business and financial results at this time.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Effective April&#160;1, 2024, pursuant to the terms of a brand ambassador agreement, we issued to an individual <span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues_c20240329__20240402__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--BrandAmbassadorAgreementMember__srt--CounterpartyNameAxis__us-gaap--IndividualMember_zt5Bv87mkxS4" title="Number of stock issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="From2024-03-292024-04-02_custom_BrandAmbassadorAgreementMember_us-gaap_IndividualMember" id="Fact000685" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">4,500</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of our common stock as compensation for the first year of the agreement. The value of the consideration transferred, measured using the fair value of our common stock at the date of issuance, was $<span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues_c20240329__20240402__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--BrandAmbassadorAgreementMember__srt--CounterpartyNameAxis__us-gaap--IndividualMember_pp0p" title="Number of total value"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues" contextRef="From2024-03-292024-04-02_custom_BrandAmbassadorAgreementMember_us-gaap_IndividualMember" id="Fact000687" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">21,690</ix:nonFraction></span>, which was recognized as expense on a straight-line basis from April&#160;1, 2024 through March&#160;31, 2025. We recognized $<span id="xdx_905_ecustom--StockGrantsExpenses_c20250101__20250331__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--BrandAmbassadorAgreementMember__srt--CounterpartyNameAxis__us-gaap--IndividualMember_pp0p" title="Stock grants expenses"><ix:nonFraction name="lucy:StockGrantsExpenses" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31_custom_BrandAmbassadorAgreementMember_us-gaap_IndividualMember" id="Fact000689" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">5,423</ix:nonFraction></span> of expense for the three months ended March&#160;31, 2025, relative to this stock grant.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Effective April&#160;1, 2025, pursuant to the terms of a brand ambassador agreement, we issued to the same individual <span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues_c20250329__20250402__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--BrandAmbassadorAgreementMember__srt--CounterpartyNameAxis__us-gaap--IndividualMember_pd" title="Number of stock issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="From2025-03-292025-04-02_custom_BrandAmbassadorAgreementMember_us-gaap_IndividualMember" id="Fact000691" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">11,539</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of our common stock as compensation for the second year of the agreement. The value of this consideration transferred, measured using the fair value of our common stock at the date of issuance, was $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues_c20250329__20250402__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--BrandAmbassadorAgreementMember__srt--CounterpartyNameAxis__us-gaap--IndividualMember_pp0p" title="Number of total value"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues" contextRef="From2025-03-292025-04-02_custom_BrandAmbassadorAgreementMember_us-gaap_IndividualMember" id="Fact000693" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">30,000</ix:nonFraction></span>, which was recognized as expense on a straight-line basis from April&#160;1, 2025 through March&#160;31, 2026. We recognized $<span id="xdx_90B_ecustom--StockGrantsExpenses_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--BrandAmbassadorAgreementMember__srt--CounterpartyNameAxis__us-gaap--IndividualMember_pp0p" title="Stock grants expenses"><ix:nonFraction name="lucy:StockGrantsExpenses" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_BrandAmbassadorAgreementMember_us-gaap_IndividualMember" id="Fact000695" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">7,500</ix:nonFraction></span> of expense for the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026, relative to this stock grant.</span></p>

Wainwright &amp; Co., LLC, as sales agent, relating to the sale of common stock. During the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026, the Company sold <span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction_c20260101__20260331__srt--CounterpartyNameAxis__custom--HCWMember__us-gaap--ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis__custom--SalesAgentMember_zERuPsM8wsF6" title="Number of common stock issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_HCWMember_custom_SalesAgentMember" id="Fact000699" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">820,800</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of common stock and received approximately $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--ProceedsFromIssuanceOfCommonStock_c20260101__20260331__srt--CounterpartyNameAxis__custom--HCWMember__us-gaap--ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis__custom--SalesAgentMember_zwqRxPe0ZReb" title="Gross proceeds before deducting sales agent commissions and offering expenses"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOfCommonStock" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_HCWMember_custom_SalesAgentMember" id="Fact000701" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,458,000</ix:nonFraction></span> of gross proceeds before deducting sales agent commissions and offering expenses. Due to the net losses for all periods presented in the unaudited condensed statements of operations, all shares underlying the common stock options, common stock warrants, and related party convertible debt were excluded from the earnings per share calculation due to their anti-dilutive effect.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt; text-transform: uppercase"><b>I</b></span><b><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><span id="a_008"></span>tem 2. Management&#8217;s Discussion and Analysis of Financial Condition and Results of Operations.</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>The following discussion and analysis of our financial condition and results of operations should be read together with our financial statements and the related notes and the other financial information included elsewhere in this Quarterly Report. This discussion contains forward-looking statements that involve risks and uncertainties. Our actual results could differ materially from those anticipated in these forward-looking statements as a result of various factors, including those discussed below and elsewhere in this Quarterly Report.</i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>Overview</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Executive Summary and Outlook</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%">
  <tr style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 0.25in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 0.25in; text-align: left"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#9679;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">We achieved strong top-line growth in the first quarter of 2026, with revenues for the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026 increasing by 70% as compared to the comparable period in 2025. This represents an acceleration from the full-year 2024 and full-year 2025 growth rates of 42% and 63%, respectively, and demonstrates continued positive momentum across the Company&#8217;s smart eyewear portfolio. The significant growth in revenues during the first quarter of 2026 reflects continued expansion of consumer demand for our products, especially our award-winning Lucyd Armor smart safety glasses line.</span></td> </tr>
  </table>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%">
  <tr style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 0.25in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 0.25in; text-align: left"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#9679;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Our gross profit margin for the first quarter of 2026 was 23%, compared to 49% in the first quarter of 2025. The first quarter of 2025 notably included the positive impact of certain credits and inventory adjustments, which did not recur in subsequent quarters, while the current quarter&#8217;s gross profit is comparable to our gross profit margins for full fiscal year 2025 and the second half of fiscal year 2025 of 21% and 22%, respectively.</span></td> </tr>

    <div style="border-bottom: Black 2pt solid; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->14<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div>
